Title: The Rise and Progress of Religion in the Soul: illustrated in a course of serious and practical addresses, suited to persons of every character and circumstance, with a devout meditation or prayer added to each chapter; by P. Doddridge, D.D.; the second edition
Creator: Philip Doddridge (1702-1751)
Publisher: London: printed for J. Brackstone, in Cornhill, and J. Waugh, in Gracechurch Street
Publication Date: 1745
Rights: Public Domain (published 1745, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item riseprogressofre00doddri (https://archive.org/details/riseprogressofre00doddri), the item's own riseprogressofre00doddri_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Columbia University Libraries; host metadata date: 1745. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: d446e5e7570ef231310a403d856cc1bdceb15c23d72eedda2e330a70c622c716.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Columbia University microfilm; the microfilm target and copyright notice of the filming library precede the title page; the text is readable with the long s as f, with noisy patches where the print shows through.
Content note: The Northampton minister and academy tutor's guide to the Christian life from first awakening to the death-bed, in thirty addresses each closed with a prayer; the census-named book of 1745 that carried Dissenting piety far beyond Dissent.
